double strike, spot, q, r, t, vol; };\nstatic const int NC=7;\n__constant__ Opt c_opts[NC];\n\n__device__ inline double cumNormal(double x){ return 0.5*(1.0+erf(x*0.7071067811865475244)); }\n__device__ double bs(const Opt& o){\n    const double S=o.spot,K=o.strike,r=o.r,q=o.q,T=o.t,sigma=o.vol;\n    if(T<=0.0||sigma<=0.0) return 0.0;\n    const double d1=(log(S/K)+(r-q+0.5*sigma*sigma)*T)/(sigma*sqrt(T));\n    const double d2=d1-sigma*sqrt(T);\n    const double Nd1=cumNormal(d1), Nd2=cumNormal(d2);\n    const double discount=exp(-r*T);\n    double price;\n    if(o.type==0) price=S*exp(-q*T)*Nd1-K*discount*Nd2;\n    else price=K*discount*cumNormal(-d2)-S*exp(-q*T)*cumNormal(-d1);\n    return price;\n}\n__global__ void kern(double* out,int n,const Opt* base){\n    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;\n    if(i>=n) return;\n    int b=i%NC;\n    Opt o=base[b];\n    double factor=1.0+0.1*(i/(double)NC);\n    o.spot*=factor; // million options/s\n    const double gpuRate = 850.0;                               // million options/s\n    double fraction = cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate);\n    fraction = std::min(0.5, std::max(0.0, fraction));\n\n    size_t cpuCount = static_cast<size_t>(static_cast<double>(count) * fraction);\n    if (cpuCount >= count && count > 0) {\n        cpuCount = count - 1;\n    }\n    const size_t gpuCount = count - cpuCount;\n\n    double* deviceResults = nullptr;\n    if (gpuCount > 0) {\n        cudaError_t status = cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceResults),\n                                        gpuCount * sizeof(double));\n        if (status != cudaSuccess) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA allocation failed: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(status));\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n\n        const unsigned int blockSize = 256;\n        const unsigned int gridSize =\n            static_cast<unsigned int>((gpuCount + blockSize - 1) / blockSize);\n        blackScholesKernel(gridSize, blockSize, start + cpuCount, deviceResults);\n        status = cudaGetLastError();\n        if (status != cudaSuccess) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA kernel launch failed: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(status));\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (cpuCount > 0) {\n        constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threads)\n        for (long long k = 0; (count == 0) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n    const double cpuRate = 10.0 * static_cast<double>(threads); // million options/s\n    const double gpuRate = 850.0;                               // * fraction);\n    if (cpuCount >= count) {\n        cpuCount = count - 1;\n    }\n    return cpuCount;\n}\n\n// Prices the global interval [start, start + count) using a device kernel while\n// OpenMP threads concurrently price a fraction of the same interval on the CPU.\n// This keeps every accelerator and the host cores busy.\nstatic void priceOptions(const size_t start, const size_t count,\n                         std::vector<double>& results, const bool materialize) {\n    results.resize(count);\n    if (count == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int threads = std::max(1, factor;\n            option.strike *= factor;\n            results[static_cast<size_t>(k)] = blackScholes(option);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (gpuCount > 0) {\n        cudaError_t status = 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType { CALL = 0, PUT = 1 };\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type; double strike; double spot; double q; double r; double t; double vol; double value; double tol;\n};\n\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept { return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2)); }\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept { return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x); }\n\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot; const double K = option.strike; const double r = option.r; const double q = option.q; const double T = option.t; const double sigma = option.vol;\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) return 0.0;\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1); const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2); const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    else price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    return price;\n}\n\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor; options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i]; const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected); const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", i, computed, expected, relError);\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed); allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n  -r           Print results for external validation\\n  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000; bool validate = false; bool printResults = false;\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) validate = true;\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) printResults = true;\n        else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); return 0; }\n        else { printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]); printUsage(argv[0]); return 1; }\n    }\n    printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options; generateOptions(options, numOptions);\n    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);\n    printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);\n    printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", duration.count() / 1000.0);\n    printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));\n    if (printResults) print_results(results, \"OptionPrices\");\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);\n        if (valid) { printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\"); return 0; }\n        else { printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\"); return 1; }\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\nEOF\ncat > /tmp/bstest/baseline/black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt
